Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sayfaSatirSayisi, sayfaNoUst, Blok2, Gen21, SonSayfa
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,ondalik,kdvsiztutar,kdvtutar,toplamtutar
Public xCizgi, Grid,zz

Sub PrintForm()
	STOK46T.SafePack()
	
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	
	siraNo = 0
	UstMargin = 6
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 32
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	' satiryuksekligi = 6
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 10
	
	kdvsiztutar = 0
	kdvtutar = 0
	toplamtutar = 0
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Siparisi-" & trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	
	SonSayfa = False
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For zz = 1 To STOK46T.GetRowCount()
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, zz)
	Next
	SonSayfa = True
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaNoYazdir(pe1)
	Call SartnameleriEkle(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	If trim(logo) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 0.5, SolMargin + 0.5, 17,-2,logo, 114)
	End If
	
	SiparisNo = STOK46E.EVRAKNO
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK46E.TARIH)
	CHKodu = STOK46E.CH_KODU
	Set C00 = Doc.Select1("e3434", "CARI00", "KOD='" & STOK46E.CH_KODU & "'", "", -1)
	CHAdi = C00.AD
	Adres1 = C00.ADRES_1
	Adres2 = C00.ADRES_2
	Adres3 = C00.ADRES_3
	Tel = C00.TELEFONNO_1
	Faks = C00.FAXNO
	email = C00.EMAIL
	ilgili = STOK46E.SIPIALAN
	
	Gen1 = 34
	Gen2 = 76
	Aralik = 6
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+20, SolMargin + 1, 10,sagmargin - solmargin, "ORDER FORM")
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+30, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Supplier Code")
	TedUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen2,": " & STOK46E.CH_KODU)
	Blok2 = pe1.GetRight + 1
	sayfaNoUst = pe1.GetTop()
	
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Supplier Name")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, Gen2,": " & CHAdi)
	
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Packing")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, Gen2,": " & trim(STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3))
	
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Palace of Delivery")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, Gen2,": " & trim(STOK46E.SEVKADRESI_1))
	
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Teknical Specs")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, Gen2,": " & trim(STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4))
	
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Payment Term")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, Gen2,": " & trim(STOK46E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1))
	
	
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Payment Condition")
'	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	
'	if trim(STOK46E.ODEMEGUNU) = "" Then
'		Odeme = "Peþin"
'	Else
'		if trim(STOK46E.FIYSEKLI) = "SP" Then
'			Odeme = "Sipariþ tarihinden itibaren "
'		Else
'			Odeme = "Fatura tarihinden itibaren "
'		End if
'		Odeme = Odeme & trim(STOK46E.ODEMEGUNU) & " gün"
'	End if

	bos = ""
	if trim(STOK46E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_2) = bos then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, Gen2,": " & Odeme)
	else
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, Gen2,": " & trim(STOK46E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_2))
	End if
	
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Notes")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, Gen2,": " & trim(STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 10, Gen1 + Gen2,trim(STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2))
	
	' Gen21 = 18
	Gen21 = 30
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(sayfaNoUst + Aralik, Blok2, 7,Gen21, "Order No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 7,2,  ": " )
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 7,sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,  trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO))
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6,Gen21, "Order Date")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK46E.TARIH))
	
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6,Gen21, "Requested From")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & trim(STOK46E.SIPIVEREN))
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6,Gen21, "Requested Responsible")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & trim(STOK46E.SIPIALAN))
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "Requested No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	talepno = ""
	Set S46T1 = Doc.Select1g("sdfagf","STOK46T1","EVRAKNO= '"& STOK46E.EVRAKNO &"'","","TALEPNO",-1)
	if S46T1.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for ll = 1 to S46T1.GetRecCount()
			talepno = talepno & " / " & trim(S46T1.TALEPNO(ll))
		Next
	End if
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & trim(talepno))
	
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "Telephone")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & Tel)
	
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "Fax")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & Faks)
	
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "e-mail")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & email)
	
	
	Str1 = "Zamanýnda teslim etmek kaydýyla, aþaðýda dökümü yapýlan malzemelerin sipariþe alýnmasýný rica ederiz" & vbcrlf & _
	"Bozuk olan mallarýn iadesi yapýlacaktýr" & vbcrlf & "Lütfen faks ile teyit ediniz."
	
	' res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 2, solMargin, -2, sagMargin - solMargin, Str1)
	
	' BaslikBasla = pe1.GetBottom + 4
	BaslikBasla = ustmargin + 84
	satirBaslangic = BaslikBasla + 6
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Sol = SolMargin
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Redim xCizgi(0)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		if i < uBound(Grid, 1) Then
			if Grid(i+1,2) = "" Then
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1)
			End if
		End if
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)
		res = pe1.write(BaslikBasla, Sol, 12, SutunGenisligi , Baslik)
		
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		if i < uBound(Grid, 1) Then
			if Grid(i+1,2) <> "" Then
				xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol
				ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)-1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	
End Sub
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	If siraNo >= sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 0
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	End If
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	' satirPozr = 4 + satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi
	satirPozr = 4 + satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi*3
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	
	
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			Degisken = STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		Else
			If DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
				Degisken = satirNo
			Else
				Degisken = ""
			End If
		End If
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		'  If Grid(i,4) = "N" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			   Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
'			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,2)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		End If
		
		If DegiskenAdi = "OR_TUTAR" Then
			if Degisken <> "" then
				kdvsiztutar = kdvsiztutar + Degisken
			End if
		elseIf DegiskenAdi = "OR_KDVTUT_EFEKTIF" Then
			if Degisken <> "" then
				kdvtutar = kdvtutar + Degisken
			End if
		elseIf DegiskenAdi = "OR_KDVDAHILTUTAR" Then
			if Degisken <> "" then
				toplamtutar = toplamtutar + Degisken
			End if
		elseIf DegiskenAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
			Degisken = Degisken & vbcrlf & trim(STOK46T.NOTE0(satirno))
		End if
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi*3, SutunGenisligi - 1.5, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
	
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	KutuUst = satirBaslangic - 7
	' KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom() + 5
	' res = pe1.box(KutuUst, SolMargin, KutuAlt - KutuUst, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	
	Set S46KUR = Doc.Select2g("asd452","STOK46T","","","PRICEUNIT",-1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom - (satiryuksekligi * 1) + 1, 60 + 10 , satiryuksekligi, 2," ") 'hizalama için
	if S46KUR.GetRecCount() > 1 then
		for z = 1 to S46KUR.GetRecCount()
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
			
			if zz = STOK46T.GetRowCount() then
				res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom , 60 + 10 , satiryuksekligi, 39, trim(S46KUR.PRICEUNIT(z)) & " Overall Total")
			Else
				res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom , 60 + 10 , satiryuksekligi, 39, trim(S46KUR.PRICEUNIT(z)) & " Sup Total")
			End if
			
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, 109, satiryuksekligi, 20, Doc.NumFormat(S46KUR.OR_TUTAR(z), 18,2))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, 109 + 20 , satiryuksekligi, 23, Doc.NumFormat(S46KUR.OR_KDVTUT_EFEKTIF(z), 18,2))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, 109 + 44, satiryuksekligi, 24, Doc.NumFormat(S46KUR.OR_KDVDAHILTUTAR(z), 18,2))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, 109 + 68, satiryuksekligi, 7, trim(S46KUR.PRICEUNIT(z)))
		Next
	else
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if zz = STOK46T.GetRowCount() then
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom , 60 + 10 , satiryuksekligi, 39, " Overall Total")
		Else
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom , 60 + 10 , satiryuksekligi, 39, " Sup Total")
		End if
		
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, 109, satiryuksekligi, 20, Doc.NumFormat(kdvsiztutar , 18,2))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, 109 + 20 , satiryuksekligi, 23, Doc.NumFormat(kdvtutar , 18,2))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, 109 + 44, satiryuksekligi, 24, Doc.NumFormat(toplamtutar , 18,2))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, 109 + 68, satiryuksekligi, 7, trim(STOK46T.PRICEUNIT(1)))
	End if
	
	
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom() - (satiryuksekligi * (S46KUR.GetRecCount-1))
	res = pe1.box(KutuUst, SolMargin, KutuAlt - KutuUst + (satiryuksekligi * (S46KUR.GetRecCount-1)), SagMargin - SolMargin)
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	
	' Str2 = "NOT: Sevk Ýrsaliyesi, fatura ve K.K raporunda ürünkodu ve sipariþ form numarasý mutlaka belirtilmelidir." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & _
	' "Sipariþlerimizin teyidini rica eder, çalýþmalarýnýzda baþarýlar dileriz." & vbcrlf & _
	' "Saygýlarýmýzla"
	
	Str2 = "Note: Shipment, billing and Quality Report and purchase order from number must be specified product code." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & _
	"We need to confirm our orders, we wish you success in your studies." & vbcrlf & _
	"Best Regards"
	
	if SonSayfa Then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 5, solMargin+1, -2, sagMargin - solMargin-1, Str2)
	End if
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 5, solMargin+1, -2, (sagMargin - solMargin-21)/2, "Genel Manager")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop , pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, (sagMargin - solMargin-21)/2, "Purchasing Manager")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 5, solMargin + 130, -2, 40, "Purchasing Manager")
	
'	if SonSayfa Then
'		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
'		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 6)
'		'  firmaunvan = trim(IDARI00.UNVAN_2) & " "& trim(IDARI00.UNVAN_1)
'		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 15, solMargin+1, satiryuksekligi-2, sagMargin - solMargin-1, "Invoice information : ")
'		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satiryuksekligi-2, sagMargin - solMargin-1, "ÜNTEL KABLOLARI SANAYÝ VE TÝCARET A.Þ.")
'		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satiryuksekligi-2, sagMargin - solMargin-1, "MAKÝNA OSB. 6. CAD. NO.4")
'		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satiryuksekligi-2, sagMargin - solMargin-1, "DÝLOVASI/KOCAELÝ")
'		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satiryuksekligi-2, sagMargin - solMargin-1, "Vergi Dairesi ve Vergi No :DIÞ TÝCARET VD. 9170016069")
'	End if
	
	' if SonSayfa Then
	'  res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 5, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)
	'  res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop +  satirYuksekligi, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)
	'  res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop +  satirYuksekligi, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)
	'  res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop +  satirYuksekligi, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)
	' End if
	
	'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 1+3, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	if S46KUR.GetRecCount() = 1 then
		res = pe1.Line(KutuAlt - (satiryuksekligi * (S46KUR.GetRecCount)) , SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	Else
		res = pe1.Line(KutuAlt - (satiryuksekligi * (S46KUR.GetRecCount-1)) , SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	End if
	
	'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To uBound(xCizgi)
		LineX = xCizgi(i)
		
		if i = 1 or i = 3 or i = 4 or i = 5 or i = 6 then
			res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, LineX, KutuAlt - KutuUst + (satiryuksekligi * (S46KUR.GetRecCount-1)), 0)
		Else
			res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, LineX, KutuAlt - KutuUst - satiryuksekligi, 0)
		End if
	Next
	
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(11,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_POS"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	
	Grid(5,0) = "OR_TUTAR"
	Grid(6,0) = "OR_KDVTUT_EFEKTIF"
	Grid(7,0) = "OR_KDVDAHILTUTAR"
	
	Grid(8,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(9,0) = "REV_RTESTARIH"
	Grid (10,0) = "NOTE0"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 5.5
	Grid(1,1) = 51
	Grid(2,1) = 17
	Grid(3,1) = 7
	Grid(4,1) = 17
	Grid(5,1) = 21
	Grid(6,1) = 24
	Grid(7,1) = 24
	
	' Grid(8,1) = 7
	Grid(8,1) = 7.5
	Grid(9,1) = 16
	' Grid(10,1) = 30
	
	Grid(0,2) = "SN"
	Grid(1,2) = "Material Description"
	Grid(2,2) = "Quantity"
	Grid(3,2) = ""
	Grid(4,2) = "Unit Price"
	
	Grid(5,2) = "Amount Of" & vbcrlf &"VAT"
	Grid(6,2) = "VAT"
	Grid(7,2) = "Total Amount"
	
	Grid(8,2) = ""
	Grid(9,2) = "Dalivery Date"
	' Grid(10,2) = "Notes"
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "N"
	
	Grid(8,4) = "C"
	Grid(9,4) = "D"
	Grid(10,4) = "C"
	
	
	Grid(0,6) = 0
	Grid(1,6) = 0
	Grid(2,6) = 0
	Grid(3,6) = 0
	Grid(4,6) = 4
	
	Grid(5,6) = 2
	Grid(6,6) = 2
	Grid(7,6) = 2
	
	Grid(8,6) = 0
	Grid(9,6) = 0
	Grid(10,6) = 0
	
'	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)

'	Next
End Sub
Sub SayfaNoYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	For i = 1 to pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(sayfaNoUst, Blok2, 5, Gen21, "Page")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount())
	Next
End Sub
Sub SartnameleriEkle(pe1)
	Set S46T = Doc.Select2G("asdfsafha", "STOK46T", "", "", "KOD", -1)
	For i = 1 to S46T.GetRowCount()
		if i > 1 Then InStr1 = InStr1 & ","
		InStr1 = InStr1 & "'" & STOK46T.KOD(i) & "'"
	Next
	Set ATC = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zsdfsadfhsj", "SELECT FULLFILENAME FROM ATCDOC WHERE XEVRAKTYPE='STOK00' AND EVRAKNO IN (" & InStr1 & ")")
	For i = 1 to ATC.GetRowCount()
		res = pe1.AddMailAttachment(trim(ATC.FULLFILENAME(i)),"")
	Next
End Sub

Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, SatirBitis
Public xCizgi, sayfaSatirSayisi, ToplamBrut, ToplamBoy,ToplamNet
Public Grid

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	UstMargin = 40
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 20
	SolMargin = 8
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 8
	satiryuksekligi = 5
'	satirBaslangic = 59
	satirBaslangic = 89
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 35
	siraNo = 0
	ToplamBrut = 0
	ToplamBoy = 0
	ToplamNet = 0

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Ceki Listesi" & trim(STOK66E.EVRAKNO), sayfaboyu,SayfaEni,"")
	STOK66T.SafePack()
	satirSayisi = STOK66T.GetRecCount()
	Call Alanlar()

	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(200,200,200))
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.Box(satirBaslangic - 14, solMargin, 13, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,255,255))
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)

	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial",8)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	ReDim xCizgi(0)
	sol = solmargin
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		res = pe1.Write(satirBaslangic - 13, Sol, 11, SutunGenisligi, Baslik)
		if Grid(i,0) = "SF_MIKTAR" Then
			SolUzunluk = sol
			GenUzunluk = SutunGenisligi
		End if
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next
	ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)-1)

	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))

	res = pe1.setalign("center")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial",11)
	carikod = STOK66E.MUSTERIKODU
	cariad = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",carikod,"")
	Adres1 = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",carikod,"")
	Adres2 = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",carikod,"")
	Adres3 = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",carikod,"")
	Telefon = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",carikod,"")
	Fax = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","FAXNO",carikod,"")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial",12)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin - 5 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin, 7, sagMargin - solMargin, "ÇEKÝ LÝSTESÝ")

	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 1, 20, 50, logo, 114)

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solmargin+140, 5, 28, "TARIH")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, 5, 30, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK66E.TARIH))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solmargin+140, 5, 28, "ÇEKÝ LÝSTESÝ NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, 5, 30, ": " & trim(STOK66E.EVRAKNO))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 54, solmargin+1, 5, 65, "FÝRMA ADI")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, 5, 100, trim(cariad))
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, pe1.GetLeft+1, 5, 100, trim(Adres1))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 100, trim(Adres2))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 100, trim(Adres3))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 50, "TEL : " & trim(Telefon))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, 5, 50, "FAX : " & Trim(Fax))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solmargin+1+65, 5, 100, "ÜNTEL KABLOLARI SAN. VE TÝC. A.Þ.")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 100, "Makine Ýml. O.S.B. 6. Cad. No:4")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 100, "41455 DÝLOVASI - KOCAELÝ / TURKEY")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial",7)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.write(UstMargin - 6 * satirYuksekligi, sagMargin - 40, 5, 40, "01.01.2008 755 F 02 Rev 2")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 25, sagMargin - 100, 5, 100, "WAREHOUSE  : " & trim(STOK66E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_1))
'	res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 25, sagMargin - 20, 5, 20, Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK66E.TARIH))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	sipno = STOK66T.OALLOC_SIPNO(satirNo)
	msipno = STOK66T.REF_TEXT_1(satirNo)
	StokKodu = STOK66T.KOD(satirNo)
	PaletNo = STOK66T.PACKNO_1(satirNo)
'	if satirNo > 1 and satirNo <> STOK66T.GetRowCount() Then
	if satirNo > 1 Then
		EskiSipStok = STOK66T.OALLOC_SIPNO(satirNo-1)&STOK66T.KOD(satirNo-1)
		EskiPaletNo = STOK66T.PACKNO_1(satirNo-1)
		if (trim(PaletNo) = "" and (sipno & StokKodu) <> EskiSipStok) or _
			(trim(PaletNo) <> "" and PaletNo <> EskiPaletNo) Then

			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)

			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, Solmargin +1, satiryuksekligi, 90, "Order No: " & trim(msipno) & "Sip.No :" & trim(sipno))
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, 9, " ")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, 38, "Total")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, 20, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBoy,18,2))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, 20, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBrut,18,2))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, Pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, 20, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamNet,18,2))
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial",9)

			siraNo = siraNo + 2
		End if
	End if
	Degisken = ""
	if siraNo > sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		Call Print_form_Arasayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()	
		siraNo = 0
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	End If

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)

	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirpozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi

	ToplamBrut = ToplamBrut + STOK66T.BRUTAGIRLIK(satirNo)
	ToplamNet = ToplamNet + STOK66T.NETAGIRLIK(satirNo)
	ToplamBoy = ToplamBoy + STOK66T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")						
	sol = solMargin + 1

	SeriNo = STOK66T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo)
	LotNo = STOK66T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)
	if InStr(1, LotNo, "/") > 0 Then
		tire = Instr(1, SeriNo, "-")
		if tire > 0 Then
			Alt = Left(SeriNo, tire - 1)
			Ust = Mid(SeriNo, tire + 1)
		Else
			Alt = ""
			ust = ""
		End if
	Else
		tire = Instr(1, LotNo, "-")
		if tire > 0 Then
			'MakaraBoyu = Doc.CDbl(trim(Mid(LotNo, tire + 1)))
		Else
			'MakaraBoyu = Doc.CDbl(trim(LotNo))
		End if
		MakaraSayisi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(STOK66T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo), MakaraBoyu))
	End if
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)

		if Left(degiskenadi, 1) <> "_" then
			Degisken = STOK66T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		Else
			if degiskenAdi = "_MSIPNO" Then
				SipArtNo = STOK66T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO(satirNo)
				Degisken = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK40E","CHSIPNO", sipno,"")
				'Degisken = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK40T", SipArtNo, "ORDERNO", 0)
			Elseif degiskenAdi = "_GRUP9" Then
				stokkodu = STOK66T.KOD(satirNo)
				Degisken = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","AD",stokkodu,"")
			Elseif degiskenAdi = "_QTYOFPAL" Then
				Degisken = 1
			Elseif degiskenAdi = "_REELLENGTH" Then
				Degisken = MakaraBoyu
			Elseif degiskenAdi = "_INNER" Then
			Degisken = Alt
			Elseif degiskenAdi = "_OUTER" Then
				Degisken = Ust
			End if
		End if
		if Grid(i,4) = "N" then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			degisken = doc.numformat(degisken, 20, Ondalik)				
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		Elseif Grid(i,4) = "NC" then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		End If
		if DegiskenAdi = "SF_MIKTAR" Then
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.NoPen()
			res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,255,0))
			res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
			res = pe1.Box(satirPozr - 1, Sol - 1, satirYuksekligi + 1, sutunGenisligi)
			res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,255,255))
			res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)
		Else
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		End if
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
	sonSatir = siraNo
End Sub
Sub Print_form_Arasayfasonu(pe1)
	EnAlt = pe1.GetBottom()

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Box(satirBaslangic - 14, solmargin, EnAlt - satirBaslangic + 14, sagMargin - solMargin)	
	
	For i = 0 to uBound(xCizgi) - 1
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, xCizgi(i), EnAlt - satirBaslangic + 14, 0)
	Next
	For i = 1 to sonSatir
		res = pe1.line(satirBaslangic + (i-1) * satiryuksekligi - 1, solmargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	next
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	EnAlt = pe1.GetBottom()

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(satirBaslangic - 14, solmargin, EnAlt - satirBaslangic + 14, sagMargin - solMargin)	
	
	For i = 0 to uBound(xCizgi) - 1
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, xCizgi(i), EnAlt - satirBaslangic + 14, 0)
	Next
	For i = 1 to sonSatir
		res = pe1.line(satirBaslangic + (i-1) * satiryuksekligi - 1, solmargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	next

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Set S66T = Doc.Select2("sdfsd", "STOK66T", "PACKNO_1<>''", "", -1)
	Set S66T2 = Doc.Select2("sd00fsd", "STOK66T", "SF_VRI_VR_R1<>''", "", -1)
'	MakaraSayisi = STOK66T.GetRowCount() - S66T.GetRowCount()
	MakaraSayisi = S66T2.GetRowCount()
	ToplamBrut = ToplamBrut + S66T.GetRowCount() * 25

	S66T.GroupBy("PACKNO_1")
	PaletSayisi = S66T.GetRowCount()
	'	ToplamStr = "Total : " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBoy, 12, 0) & " m, " & MakaraSayisi & " drums, " & PaletSayisi & " pallets, " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBrut,12,0) & " kg"
	ToplamStr = "Total : " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBoy, 12, 0) & " m, " & MakaraSayisi & " drums, " & PaletSayisi & " pallets "
	ToplamStr1 = "Brüt : "& Doc.NumFormat(ToplamBrut,12,0) & " kg"

	res = pe1.Write(EnAlt + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, 100, ToplamStr)
	res = pe1.Write(EnAlt + satirYuksekligi+5, solMargin, 7, 100, ToplamStr1)
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, 150, STOK66E.NOTES_3)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, 150, STOK66E.NOTES_4)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(8,7)

	Grid(0,0) = "PACKNO_1"
	Grid(1,0) = "REF_TEXT_1"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_VRI_VR_R1"
	Grid(4,0) = "LOTNUMBER"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(6,0) = "BRUTAGIRLIK"
	Grid(7,0) = "NETAGIRLIK"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 12
	Grid(1,1) = 12
	Grid(2,1) = 75
	Grid(3,1) = 15
	Grid(4,1) = 20
	Grid(5,1) = 20
	Grid(6,1) = 20
	Grid(7,1) = 20

	Grid(0,2) = "PALET NO"
	Grid(1,2) = "POZ NO"
	Grid(2,2) = "STOK ADI"
	Grid(3,2) = "MAKARARA TÝPÝ"
	Grid(4,2) = "LOT NO"
	Grid(5,2)	 = "TOPLAM (m)"
	Grid(6,2)	 = "BRÜT AÐR."
	Grid(7,2)	 = "NET AÐR."

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "N"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			
'			ondalik = STOK66T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)
			ondalik = 2
		Grid(i,6) = Ondalik				
		End If
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2013 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : IRSALIYE-1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK61
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : SEVK ÝRSALÝYESÝ
Kod : 
Dim satirAraligi, UstMargin, SolMargin, 	SayfaEni, AltMargin, SayfaBoyu, SagMargin, S60T
Dim Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	SayfaBoyu = 297
	SayfaEni  = 210
	SolMargin = 8
	satirAraligi = 4.3
	UstMargin = 7 * satirAraligi

	STOK61T.SafePack()
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2("dsfgedhsj", "STOK61T", "", "", -1)
	S60T.GroupBy2("KOD")

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"UNT - SEVK ÝRSALÝYESÝ", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni,"")

	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(6) 
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage_P(66)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetFormLength(11)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(17)

		Call Alanlar(pe1)	
		Call Ust_Bolum(pe1)	
		For i = 1 To S60T.GetRecCount() 
			Call Satir_Yaz(pe1, i) 	
		Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Ust_Bolum(pe1)

	MusteriCariKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
	EvrakNo =  STOK60E.EVRAKNO
	Musteri = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "AD" , MusteriCariKodu , "")
	
	ch_sipno = trim(Doc.GetdbFieldValue("STOK40E" , "CHSIPNO" , S60T.SIPNO(1) , ""))

	sevkadresi1 = trim(STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1)
	sevkadresi2 = trim(STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_2)
	sevkadresi3 = trim(STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_3)
	
	sekad = sevkadresi1 & sevkadresi2 & sevkadresi3

	if sekad <> "" then
		MusterAdresi1 = sevkadresi1 
		MusterAdresi2 = sevkadresi2
		MusterAdresi3 = sevkadresi3		
	else
		MusterAdresi1 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "ADRES_1" , MusteriCariKodu , "")
		MusterAdresi2 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "ADRES_2" , MusteriCariKodu , "")
		MusterAdresi3 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "ADRES_3" , MusteriCariKodu , "")
	end if

	EvrakTarihi = STOK60E.TARIH
	MusteriVergiDairesi = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "VERGIDAIRESI" , MusteriCariKodu , "")
	MusteriVergiNo = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "VERGINO" , MusteriCariKodu , "")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, SolMargin , satirAraligi, 95 , Trim(Musteri))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi, SolMargin, satirAraligi, 95 , Trim(MusterAdresi1))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 2, SolMargin, satirAraligi, 65 , Trim(MusterAdresi2))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 3, SolMargin, satirAraligi, 65 , Trim(MusterAdresi3))

	sag_konum = 145
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 4, SolMargin + sag_konum , satirAraligi, 25 , Doc.StrToNorDate(EvrakTarihi))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 5, SolMargin + sag_konum , satirAraligi, 25 , Doc.StrToNorDate(EvrakTarihi))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 7, SolMargin + sag_konum , satirAraligi, 25 , ch_sipno)

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 7, SolMargin + 25 , satirAraligi, 60 , Trim(MusteriVergiDairesi) & " " & Trim(MusteriVergiNo))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 46, SolMargin + 15 , satirAraligi, 60 , Trim(STOK60E.BOX1_1))
End Sub

Sub Satir_Yaz(pe1, satirno) 
	Dim satir_bilgisi, saga_kayma, Flag_saga_devam_mi 
	if satirno = 1 Then
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 13  , Solmargin - 5 , satirAraligi , baslangic, satir_bilgisi) 		
	End if
	if satirno > 1 Then
	 res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,Solmargin - 5 ,1,baslangic, "") '// Hizalama iþlemi
	End if
	Flag_saga_devam_mi = 0

	For i = 0 To Ubound (Grid,2)
		satir_bilgisi = S60T.GetFieldValue(Grid(2, i) ,satirno)

		if Trim(Grid(2, i)) = "NO" Then  satir_bilgisi = satirno 
			baslangic = Grid(1,i)
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if Grid(3,i) = "N" And 	Flag_saga_devam_mi = 0 Then '// En baþ sütuna rakamsal deðer text olarak basýlacak
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			saga_kayma = -2
			Flag_saga_devam_mi = 1  
		Elseif Grid(3,i) = "N" And Flag_saga_devam_mi = 1 Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			saga_kayma = 0 
			Flag_saga_devam_mi = 1  
		Elseif Grid(3,i) <> "N" And Flag_saga_devam_mi = 1 Then
			saga_kayma = 2
			Flag_saga_devam_mi = 0
		End if
		if Grid(3,i) = "N" Then
			satir_bilgisi = Doc.numformat_NLZ(satir_bilgisi, 16,Grid(4,i))			
		Else
			satir_bilgisi = Trim(satir_bilgisi)
		End if

		if trim(Grid(2, i)) = "SF_MIKTAR" Then
			satir_bilgisi = satir_bilgisi & " " & trim(S60T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirno))
		End if 

		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop  , pe1.GetRight + saga_kayma , satirAraligi , baslangic, satir_bilgisi)
		saga_kayma = 0
	Next

	if pe1.GetTop() > UstMargin + satirAraligi * 36 And satirno < S60T.GetRecCount() Then
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Ust_Bolum(pe1)
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 11, 1,1,1, "") '// Hizalama iþlemi
	End if
End Sub
Sub Alanlar(pe1)
	ReDim Grid(4,6)
'// Alan Geniþlikleri
	Grid(1,0) = 40
	Grid(1,1) = 20 
	Grid(1,2) = 60
	Grid(1,3) = 25
	Grid(1,4) = 50
'// Satýr Ýþlemleri
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,1) = "SIPNO"
	Grid(2,2) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,3) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(2,4) = "NOTES"
'// Hizalama
	Grid(3,0) = "T"
	Grid(3,1) = "T"
	Grid(3,2) = "T"
	Grid(3,3) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "T"
'// Ondalýk
	Grid(4,0) = 0
	Grid(4,1) = 0
	Grid(4,2) = 0
	Grid(4,3) = 2
End Sub

	end if

	MusteriVergiDairesi = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00" , "VERGIDAIRESI" , MusteriCariKodu , "")

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 4, SolMargin + sag_konum , satirAraligi, 25 , Doc.StrToNorDate(EvrakTarihi))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 5, SolMargin + sag_konum , satirAraligi, 25 , Doc.StrToNorDate(EvrakTarihi))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 7, SolMargin + sag_konum , satirAraligi, 25 , ch_sipno)

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 46, SolMargin + 15 , satirAraligi, 60 , Trim(STOK60E.BOX1_1))
End Sub

Sub Satir_Yaz(pe1, satirno) 
	Dim satir_bilgisi, saga_kayma, Flag_saga_devam_mi 
	if satirno = 1 Then
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 13  , Solmargin - 5 , satirAraligi , baslangic, satir_bilgisi) 		
	End if
	if satirno > 1 Then
	 res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop + satirAraligi ,Solmargin - 5 ,1,baslangic, "") '// Hizalama iþlemi
	End if
	Flag_saga_devam_mi = 0

	For i = 0 To Ubound (Grid,2)
		satir_bilgisi = S60T.GetFieldValue(Grid(2, i) ,satirno)

		if Trim(Grid(2, i)) = "NO" Then  satir_bilgisi = satirno 
			baslangic = Grid(1,i)
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if Grid(3,i) = "N" And 	Flag_saga_devam_mi = 0 Then '// En baþ sütuna rakamsal deðer text olarak basýlacak
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			saga_kayma = -2
			Flag_saga_devam_mi = 1  
		Elseif Grid(3,i) = "N" And Flag_saga_devam_mi = 1 Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			saga_kayma = 0 
			Flag_saga_devam_mi = 1  
		Elseif Grid(3,i) <> "N" And Flag_saga_devam_mi = 1 Then
			saga_kayma = 2
			Flag_saga_devam_mi = 0
		End if
		if Grid(3,i) = "N" Then
			satir_bilgisi = Doc.numformat_NLZ(satir_bilgisi, 16,Grid(4,i))			
		Else
			satir_bilgisi = Trim(satir_bilgisi)
		End if

		if trim(Grid(2, i)) = "SF_MIKTAR" Then
			satir_bilgisi = satir_bilgisi & " " & trim(S60T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirno))
		End if 

		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop  , pe1.GetRight + saga_kayma , satirAraligi , baslangic, satir_bilgisi)
		saga_kayma = 0
	Next

	if pe1.GetTop() > UstMargin + satirAraligi * 36 And satirno < S60T.GetRecCount() Then
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Ust_Bolum(pe1)
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirAraligi * 11, 1,1,1, "") '// Hizalama iþlemi
	End if
End Sub
Sub Alanlar(pe1)
	ReDim Grid(4,6)
'// Alan Geniþlikleri
	Grid(1,0) = 40
	Grid(1,1) = 20 
	Grid(1,2) = 60
	Grid(1,3) = 25
	Grid(1,4) = 50
'// Satýr Ýþlemleri
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,1) = "SIPNO"
	Grid(2,2) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,3) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(2,4) = "NOTES"
'// Hizalama
	Grid(3,0) = "T"
	Grid(3,1) = "T"
	Grid(3,2) = "T"
	Grid(3,3) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "T"
'// Ondalýk
	Grid(4,0) = 0
	Grid(4,1) = 0
	Grid(4,2) = 0
	Grid(4,3) = 2
End Sub                


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2013 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK47-2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK47
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Satýnalma Talebi
Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,UstMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, Y5, sayfaSatirSayisi
Public Grid, xCizgi, sayfaNo
Sub PrintForm()
	siraNo = 0
	sayfaNo = 0 

	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 10
	solMargin = 8
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 8
	satiryuksekligi = 7
	SiraNo = 0
	sayfaSatirsayisi = 28
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	Call Alanlar()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Talebi", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	Dim satirSayisi_tableT
	satirSayisi_tableT = STOK47T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, I)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	ReDim xCizgi(0)
	D1 = solMargin + 120
	D2 = solMargin + 145
	Y1 = UstMargin + 21
	Y2 = UstMargin + 28
	Y3 = UstMargin + 35
	Y4 = UstMargin + 42
	Y5 = UstMargin + 44
 
	res = pe1.Box(Y5,solMargin,altMargin - Y5,sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 18)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 3, solMargin + 60, 15, sagMargin - solMargin - 120, "SATINALMA TALEBÝ")
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Picture(Ustmargin + 1, solMargin + 1, Y1 - UstMargin - 2, 55, logo, 114)

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "9")
'*******************

	BBasla = UstMargin + 15
	SatirAra = 7
	Genislik10 = 27
	Genislik20 = 30

	FisNo = STOK47E.EVRAKNO(1)
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK47E.TARIH(1))
	TalepEdenBolum = STOK47E.CODEDBOLUM(1)
	TalepEdenBolumAdi = doc.GetGecoValue("DEPT","AD",TalepEdenBolum ,-1)
	TalepEdenKisi = STOK47E.CODEDKISI(1)  
	TalepEden = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00","AD",TalepEdenKisi,"")

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 3, D2+1, 5,sagMargin - D2 - 2, "FORM KODU : ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 10.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5,sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft - 2, "REVÝZYON : ")

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(Y1 + 1 , SolMargin+1, 6, Genislik10, "ÝSTEÐÝ YAPAN")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, D1 - pe1.getRight - 2, ": " & Trim(TalepEden))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , D1 + 1, 6, Genislik20, "ÝSTEK NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2, ": " & trim(FisNo))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(Y2 + 1, SolMargin+1, 6, Genislik10, "BÖLÜM ADI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, D1 - pe1.getRight - 2, ": " & Trim(TalepEdenBolumAdi))		
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , D1 + 1, 6, Genislik20, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2, ": " & Tarih)				

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(Y3 + 1 , SolMargin+1, 6, Genislik10, "ONAY")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, D1 - pe1.getRight - 2, ": ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , D1 + 1, 6, Genislik20, "ONAY")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2, ": ")
	
'***********************		
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom + 18

	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)
	Sol = solmargin + 0.5
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
			if i = 3 then
				Genislik = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i + 1,1) + Grid(i + 2,1)
				res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 14, Sol, 10, Genislik - 1, "ÝSTENEN")
				res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 8,sol - 0.5, 0, Genislik)
				Ust = satirBaslangic - 7
			elseif i = 4 or i = 5 then
				Ust = satirBaslangic - 7
			else
				Ust  = satirBaslangic - 14
			end if
			res = pe1.write(Ust, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 1, Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)) =	Sol - 0.5
			redim Preserve xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)+1)
		End If
	Next

'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solmargin, Y4 - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, solmargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, solmargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, solmargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin, D2, Y1 - UstMargin, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, D1, Y4 - Y1, 0)

End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	if trim(STOK47T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" then exit sub
	SiraNo = SiraNo + 1
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If SiraNo > sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		SiraNo = 1
		sayfaNo = sayfaNo + 1
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi
		End If
	End If
		
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = solMargin + 0.5
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		if DegiskenAdi = "_CINS" then
			StokKodu = STOK47T.KOD(satirNo)
			Stokadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
			Degisken = trim(StokAdi)
		Elseif DegiskenAdi = "_SN" then
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			Degisken = SiraNo + sayfaNo * sayfaSatirSayisi
		else
			Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
		end if
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "CC" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		End If
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then
			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 1, Degisken)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		End If	
		Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

'dikey çizgiler
	cizgiAlt = satirBaslangic - 1 + sayfaSatirSayisi * satirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi)- 2
	  LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		if i = 3 or i = 4 then
		  res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 8, LineX, cizgiAlt - satirBaslangic + 8, 0)
		else
		  res = pe1.Line(Y5, LineX, cizgiAlt - Y5, 0)
		end if
	Next
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 1,solMargin,0,sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(cizgiAlt,solMargin,0,sagMargin - solMargin)
'yatay çizgiler	
	res = pe1.pensize(0.1)
	for i = 1 to sayfaSatirSayisi
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 1 + i * satirYuksekligi,solMargin,0,sagMargin - solMargin)	
	Next
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Notlar1 = STOK47E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1
	Notlar2 = STOK47E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2
	res = pe1.Write(cizgiAlt + 1,solMargin + 1,6,35,"GENEL AÇIKLAMA :")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,6,sagmargin - pe1.GetRight - 2,Notlar1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft,6,sagmargin - pe1.GetLeft - 2,Notlar2)


	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(cizgiAlt + 15,solMargin + 1,6,45,"TALEP EDEN :")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,pe1.GetRight + 55,6,35,"ONAYLAYAN :")
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(7,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SN"
	Grid(1,0) = "_CINS"
	Grid(2,0) = "NOTE0"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(5,0) = "RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH"
	Grid(6,0) = "_ONAYLANAN"
	Grid(7,0) = "_TAKIP"

	Grid(0,1) = 8
	Grid(1,1) = 62
	Grid(2,1) = 55
	Grid(3,1) = 18
	Grid(4,1) = 7
	Grid(5,1) = 16
	Grid(6,1) = 18
	Grid(7,1) = 12

	Grid(0,2) = "SIRA NO"
	Grid(1,2) = "ÝSTENEN MALZEMENÝN TANIMI"
	Grid(2,2) = "AÇIKLAMA"
	Grid(3,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(4,2) = "BR."
	Grid(5,2) = "TARÝH"
	Grid(6,2) = "ONAY MÝKTARI"
	Grid(7,2) = "Takip"
	
	Grid(0,4) = "CC"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "D"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK47T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.setitalic(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
	res = pe1.setrotation(0)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2013 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : MPS
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10

Açýklama : Ýþ Emri Örneði

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, M10TI, M10TH, EnAlt, ML, ML1, M10TM, M10TM1
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi, hmdSatirBasla, MuhendislikSatirBasla
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic

Sub PrintForm()
	if trim(MMPS10E.STATUS) <> "R" Then
		MMPS10E.STATUS = "R"
	End if

	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "FX" Then Doc.Save_Voucher

	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	
	siraNo = 0
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 14
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 8
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirSonu = AltMargin - 10
	EnAlt = 0

	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Ýþ Emri - " & trim(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	par1 = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
	par2 = ""
	Set ML = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.BAGLANTI", "GetKaynakMPSList", par1, par2)
	Set ML1 = Doc.Select2("dfr5t456", ML.GetAppName, "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='SP'","",-1)

	Set M10TI = Doc.Select2("sese222", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)
	satirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	For i = 1 to M10TI.GetRowCount()
		if i > 1 Then
			Call BildirimGiris(pe1)
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			pe1.PageBreak
		End if
		Call IsEmriPrint(pe1, i)
	Next
	satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom()
	Call BildirimGiris(pe1)
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'	Call SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TI.GetAppName
	Doc.DeleteTableObject ML.GetAppName
	Doc.DeleteTableObject ML1.GetAppName
End Sub
Sub IsEmriPrint(pe1, OpSatirNo)
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, OpSatirNo)

	Set M10TH = Doc.Select2("de3434r", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_SIRANO='" & M10TI.R_SIRANO(OpSatirNo) & "'", "", -1) 

	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("3Rd0e340Gr", "MMPS10TM", "KAYNAKMPS='"& M10TH.EVRAKNO &"'", "", -1)

'	Call SendTableToExcel(M10TM)
	siraNo = 0        
	Call MuhendislikTanimiYaz(pe1, OpsatirNo)
	For i = 1 To M10TH.GetRowCount()
		Call HammaddeYaz(pe1, i, OpSatirNo)
	Next
'	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TH.GetAppName
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, OpSatirNo)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin, solMargin, 15, 50, logo, 114) 

	MamulMiktari = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
	Ondalik = Doc.SigDig(MamulMiktari)
	MamulMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(MamulMiktari,18,Ondalik)
	StokBirimi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",MamulKodu,"")

	MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU
	A = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","GK_1",MusteriKodu,"")
	MusteriAdi = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK1","AD",A,-1)
	Not1 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1
	Not2 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_2

	TezgahKodu = M10TI.R_KAYNAKKODU(OpSatirNo)
	Tezgahlar = trim(TezgahKodu)
	AlternatifTezgahlar = GetAlternatifTezgah(M10TI.R_MAMULKODU(OpSatirNo), M10TI.ALTERNATIFKODU(OpSatirNo))
	if trim(AlternatifTezgahlar) <> "" Then
		Tezgahlar = Tezgahlar & " (" & AlternatifTezgahlar & ")"
	End if
	TezgahAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","AD",TezgahKodu,"")
	OperasyonKodu = M10TI.R_OPERASYON(OpSatirNo)
	OperasyonAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01","AD",OperasyonKodu,"")
	MamulPaket = M10TI.R_YMK_MPAKET(OpSatirNo)
	YMamulPaket = M10TI.R_YMK_YMPAKET(OpSatirNo)
	YMPaketIcerigi = M10TI.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI(OpSatirNo)
	YMMiktari = M10TI.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(OpSatirNo)
	OncekiOp = GetOncekiOp(M10TI, OpSatirNo)
	SonrakiOp = GetSonrakiOp(M10TI, OpSatirNo)

	YMPaketIcerigi = Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(YMPaketIcerigi,18,6)
	PlanlananHiz = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(YMMiktari, M10TI.R_MIKTAR0(OpSatirNo) * 60), 1)

	FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
'	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin - 5,SolMargin, 6,180,FirmaAdi)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	Gen10 = 28
	Gen11 = 85
	Gen20 = 33

	GrupNo = MMPS10E.GRUPEVRAKNO
	ToleransTipi = MMPS10E.TOLERANSTYPE
	Tolerans = MMPS10E.TOLERANS

	bBasla = UstMargin + 7
	SatirAra = 6
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin, 6, sagMargin - solmargin, trim(OperasyonAdi) & " Ýþ Emri")

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra + 3, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Tezgah")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & Tezgahlar)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen20, "Termin")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & Doc.StrtoNorDate(MMPS10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH))

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Ýþ Emri No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & trim(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen20, "Ýþ No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & trim(M10TI.EVRAKNO(OpSatirNo)) & "/" & Doc.CDbl(M10TI.TRNUM(OpSatirNo)))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Ýþ Emri Miktarý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & trim(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(M10TI.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(OpSatirNo),18,0)) & " " & trim(MMPS10E.SF_SF_UNIT))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6,Gen20, "Önceki Operasyon")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", OncekiOp, ""))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Planlanan Hýz")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 30,": " & PlanlananHiz & " m/dk")

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 6, 6,Gen10, "Gerçekleþen Hýz")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 20,": ")

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6,Gen20, "Sonraki Operasyon")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", SonrakiOp, ""))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	if Left(MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 1) = "3" Then
		MamulYarimamul = "Mamul"
	Else
		MamulYarimamul = "Yarýmamul"
	End if
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6, Gen10, MamulYarimamul & " Kodu")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6,Gen20, "Ýþ Emri Toleransý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	tolerans = MMPS10E.TOLERANS
	if tolerans = 0 then tolerans = ""
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & MMPS10E.TOLERANSTYPE & " " &tolerans)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, MamulYarimamul & " Adý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")) & " "&trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "NAME2", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Lot No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": ")

	Alt = pe1.GetBottom()

	TMBasla = 0
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	satirAra1 = 4 
	if trim(MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(1)) = "UP" and Left(MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 3) <> "202" Then
		Gen1 = 95
		Gen2 = 25
		Gen4 = 30

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Kaynak YarýMamul/Mamul")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Kaynak MPS")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen4, "Kaynak MPS Mik.")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen4, "Kaynak MPS Tol.")

		KutuUst = pe1.GetTop() - 0.5
		KutuSag = pe1.GetRight
		TMBasla = pe1.GetTop + satirAra - 0.5

		MMPS10TM.SafePack()
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		For i = 1 to MMPS10TM.GetRowCount()
			satirPos = TMBasla + (i - 1) * satirAra1
			KaynakStokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "MAMULSTOKKODU", MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i), "")
			Toleranstipi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "TOLERANSTYPE", MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i), "")
			Toleransorani = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "TOLERANS", MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i), "")
			if Toleransorani = 0 then Toleransorani = ""

			res = pe1.Write(satirPos, solMargin + 1, 4, Gen1, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", KaynakStokKodu, ""))
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4, Gen2, trim(MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i)))
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4, Gen4-5, Doc.NumFormat(MMPS10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i), 12, 0))
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 4, Gen4-4, Toleranstipi &" " &Toleransorani)

			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		Next
		Alt = pe1.GetBottom()

		res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
		res = pe1.Line(TMBasla - 0.5, SolMargin, 0, KutuSag - SolMargin)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, SolMargin + Gen1, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen2, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen3, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen4, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Box(KutuUst, SolMargin, Alt - KutuUst, KutuSag - SolMargin)
		EnAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	End if

	if Left(MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 3) <> "202" Then
		Gen1 = 24
		Gen2 = 18
		Gen3 = 80
		Gen4 = 25
		Gen5 = 44

		if TMBasla = 0 Then
			Basla1 = pe1.GetTop + satirAra - 1
		Else
			Basla1 = pe1.GetBottom + 2
		End if
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla1, solMargin + 1, 4, Gen1, "Müþteri")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 4, Gen2, "Sipariþ No")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 4, Gen3, "Mamul")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 4, Gen4, "Sevk Boyu")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 4, Gen5, "Sipariþ Notu")

		KutuUst = pe1.GetTop() - 0.5
		KutuSag = pe1.GetRight
		TMBasla = pe1.GetTop + satirAra - 0.5
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		For i = 1 to ML1.GetRowCount()
			satirPos = TMBasla + (i - 1) * satirAra1
			Set S40T = Doc.Select1("sefsf4fsdgdfg", "STOK40T", "EVRAKNO='" &  ML1.KAYNAKMPS(i) & "'&TRNUM='" & ML1.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if S40T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				CHKodu = S40T.MUSTERIKODU(1)
				SipPaketMiktari = S40T.SF_PMIKTAR(1)
				SipPaketIcerigi = S40T.SF_IMIKTAR(1)
				if trim(S40T.TOLERANSTYPE(1)) <> "" Then
					Tolerans = trim(S40T.TOLERANSTYPE(1)) & " % " & S40T.TOLERANS(1)
				Else
					Tolerans = ""
				End if
				SipMiktari = SipPaketMiktari & " x " & trim(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(SipPaketIcerigi, 12,6)) & " " & Tolerans
				SiparisNotu = S40T.NOTES(1)
				HedefMamulKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "KOD", ML1.KAYNAKMPS(i) & ML1.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(i), "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
				HedefMamulAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", HedefMamulKodu, "")
			

				res = pe1.Write(satirPos, solMargin + 1, 4, Gen1 - 2, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, ""))
				res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 4, Gen2 - 2, trim(ML1.KAYNAKMPS(i)))
				res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 4, Gen3 - 2, HedefMamulAdi)
				res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 4, Gen4 - 2, SipMiktari)
				res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 4, Gen5 - 2, SiparisNotu)
			End if
		Next
		Alt = pe1.GetBottom()
		res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
		res = pe1.Line(TMBasla - 0.5, SolMargin, 0, KutuSag - SolMargin)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, SolMargin + Gen1, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen2, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen3, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen4, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Box(KutuUst, SolMargin, Alt - KutuUst, KutuSag - SolMargin)
		EnAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	End if
	hmdSatirBasla = Alt + satirYuksekligi
	MuhendislikSatirBasla = Alt + 2
	if IsObject(S40T) Then Doc.DeleteTableObject S40T.GetAppName
End Sub
Sub MuhendislikTanimiYaz(pe1, OpSatirNo)
	MamulKodu = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU
	YariMamulKodu = M10TI.R_YMAMULKODU(OpSatirNo)
	KaynakTipi = M10TI.R_KAYNAKTYPE(OpSatirNo)
	KaynakKodu = M10TI.R_KAYNAKKODU(OpSatirNo)
	OperasyonKodu = M10TI.R_OPERASYON(OpSatirNo)
	MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU
	MPSNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
	
	res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu,MusteriKodu,MPSNo)
	Set Tech = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT")
	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "FX" Then
		Doc.RunMacro1 "MAKR7S.EXCELOUT", "SendTableToExcel", Tech
	End if
	if Tech.GetRecCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(MuhendislikSatirBasla, solMargin, 5, 50, "Mühendislik Tanýmlarý")
	MuhSatirBasla = MuhendislikSatirBasla + satirYuksekligi + 0.5
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Gen1 = 60
	Gen2 = 45
	Gen3 = 44
	res = pe1.Write(MuhSatirBasla, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Parametre")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Deðer")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen3, "Gerçekleþen Deðer")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, "K. Kontrol Onayý")

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	MarkalamaUst = 0
	resimUst = 0
	For i = 1 To Tech.GetRecCount()
		if i = 1 Then
			satirPozr = MuhSatirBasla + Satiryuksekligi
		Else
			satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom + 1
		End if
		if trim(Tech.TSCODE(i)) = "RESIM" Then
			DosyaAdi = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & "IMAGES\" & trim(Tech.TSTEXT(i))
			sol = solMargin + Gen1 + 1
			res = pe1.NoPen()
			res = pe1.Picture(SatirPozr, solMargin + 1, -2, sagMargin - solMargin - 2, DosyaAdi, 114)
			resimUst = pe1.GetTop()
		Else
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, Doc.GetGecoValue("TECHSC", "AD", Tech.TSCODE(i), -1))
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, trim(Tech.TSTEXT(i)))
			if trim(Tech.TSCODE(i)) = "MARKALAMA" and MarkalamaUst = 0 Then
				MarkalamaUst = pe1.GetTop()
			End if
		End if
	Next
	MuhSatirBasla1 = pe1.GetBottom + 1
	if MuhSatirBasla1 < MuhSatirBasla Then MuhSatirBasla1 = MuhSatirBasla
	For j = 1 to ML1.GetRowCount()
		MamulKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "KOD", ML1.KAYNAKMPS(j) & ML1.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(j), "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
		if trim(MamulKodu) <> trim(MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU) Then
			Set Tech = GetMamulMuhendislikTanimi(MamulKodu, OpSatirNo)
			For i = 1 To Tech.GetRecCount()
				if i = 1 and j = 1 Then
					satirPozr = MuhSatirBasla1
				Else
					satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom + 1
				End if
				if trim(Tech.TSCODE(i)) = "RESIM" Then
					DosyaAdi = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & "IMAGES\" & trim(Tech.TSTEXT(i))
					sol = solMargin + Gen1 + 1
					res = pe1.NoPen()
					res = pe1.Picture(SatirPozr, sol, -2, sagMargin - sol - 1, DosyaAdi, 114)
					resimUst = pe1.GetTop()
				Else
					TSCode = trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("TECHSC", "AD", Tech.TSCODE(i), -1)) & " - " & _
							trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MamulKodu, ""))

					res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, TSCode)
					res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, trim(Tech.TSTEXT(i)))
				End if
			Next
		End if
	Next
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	KutuSag = pe1.GetRight()
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(MuhSatirBasla - 1, solMargin, KutuAlt - MuhSatirBasla + 1, KutuSag - solMargin)
	EnAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	hmdSatirBasla = pe1.GetBottom() + 2
	res = pe1.Line(MuhSatirBasla + satirYuksekligi - 0.4, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
	cizgiAlt = KutuAlt
	cizgiAlt1 = cizgiAlt
	if resimUst > 0 Then CizgiAlt = ResimUst
	if MarkalamaUst > 0 and MarkalamaUst < CizgiAlt Then CizgiAlt1 = MarkalamaUst
	res = pe1.Line(MuhSatirBasla -1, solMargin + Gen1, cizgiAlt - MuhSatirBasla + 1, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(MuhSatirBasla -1, pe1.GetLeft + Gen2, cizgiAlt1 - MuhSatirBasla + 1, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(MuhSatirBasla -1, pe1.GetLeft + Gen3, cizgiAlt1 - MuhSatirBasla + 1, 0)
End Sub
Function GetMamulMuhendislikTanimi(MamulKodu, OpSatirNo)
'	YariMamulKodu = M10TI.R_YMAMULKODU(OpSatirNo)
	KaynakTipi = M10TI.R_KAYNAKTYPE(OpSatirNo)
	KaynakKodu = M10TI.R_KAYNAKKODU(OpSatirNo)
	OperasyonKodu = M10TI.R_OPERASYON(OpSatirNo)
	MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU
	MPSNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
	
	res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu,MusteriKodu,MPSNo)
	Set GetMamulMuhendislikTanimi = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT")
End Function
Sub HammaddeYaz(pe1, satirNo, OpSatirNo)
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	SatirPozr = hmdSatirBasla + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi

	HammaddeKodu = M10TH.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo)
	HammaddeAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",HammaddeKodu,"")
	HammaddeMiktari = M10TH.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo)	
	HammaddeBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",HammaddeKodu,"")

	for lk=1 to M10TM.GetRecCount()
		if M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(lk) = HammaddeKodu then YarimamulMPS = M10TM.EVRAKNO(lk)
	next

	Gen1 = 35
	Gen2 = 84
	Gen3 = 17
	Gen4 = 9
	Gen5 = 17
	Gen6 = 11
	Gen7 = 19
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	if satirNo = 1 Then
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,SolMargin + 1, 5,Gen1, "Hammadde Kodu")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen2, "Hammadde Adý")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen3, "Miktar")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen4, "Birim")	
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen5, "1000 m sarf.")	
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen6, "Lot No")	
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen7, "Ýþ Emri No")	
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
		SatirPozr = hmdSatirBasla + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	End if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,SolMargin + 1, 5,Gen1 - 1.5, HammaddeKodu)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight + 1.5, 5,Gen2 - 1.5, HammaddeAdi)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	Ondalik = M10TH.MaxSigDig("R_MIKTAR0")
	if Ondalik > 3 Then Ondalik = 3
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr + 0.4,pe1.GetRight + 1.5, 5,Gen3 - 1.5, Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(HammaddeMiktari, 12,Ondalik))
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight + 1.5, 5,Gen4 - 1.5, HammaddeBirim)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr + 0.4,pe1.GetRight + 1.5, 5,Gen5 - 1.5, Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Doc.Div(HammaddeMiktari * 1000, MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR), 12,Ondalik))
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight + 1.5, 5,Gen6 - 1.5, "")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight + 1.5, 5,Gen7 - 1.5, YarimamulMPS)

	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	KutuSag = pe1.GetRight()
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	if satirNo = M10TH.GetRowCount() Then
		res = pe1.Box(hmdSatirBasla - 1, solMargin, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, KutuSag - solMargin)
		EnAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, solMargin + Gen1, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, pe1.GetLeft + Gen2, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, pe1.GetLeft + Gen3, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, pe1.GetLeft + Gen4, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, pe1.GetLeft + Gen5, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, pe1.GetLeft + Gen6, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1 + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
	End if
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin + 0.5, solMargin,5,100,"Form No:300/FRM/010 Rev.2 09.03.2010")
End Sub
Sub BildirimGiris(pe1)
	GenArr = Array(20, 10, 37.2, 15, 15, 35, 23, 18.3, 18.3)
	Basla = AltMargin - 85
	Bitis = AltMargin
	if EnAlt > Basla Then
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Basla = UstMargin
		Bitis = Basla + 85
	End If
	satirAra = 4.7
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Basla + 1, solMargin, 10, GenArr(0), "Tarih")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 10, GenArr(1), "Proses Kodu")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 10, GenArr(2), "Operatör")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 10, GenArr(3), "Baþlangýç Saati")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 10, GenArr(4), "Bitiþ Saati")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 10, GenArr(5), "Kesilen Hurda Miktarý (m)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 10, GenArr(6), "Makara No" & vbcrlf & "(Lot No)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 10, GenArr(7), "Üretim Miktarý")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 10, GenArr(8), "K.K. Gözlem Noktasý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	res = pe1.Box(Basla, solMargin, Bitis - Basla, sagMargin - solMargin)
	sol = solMargin
	For i = 0 to uBound(GenArr) - 1
		sol = sol + GenArr(i)
		res = pe1.Line(Basla, sol, Bitis - Basla, 0)
	Next
	CizgiBasla = Basla + 9
	satirSayisi = Fix((Bitis - CizgiBasla) / satirAra)
	For i = 1 to satirSayisi
		res = pe1.Line(CizgiBasla + (i-1) * satirAra, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Hazirlayan(pe1,SatirPozr)
	If (satirPozr + 30) > satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		satirPozr = UstMargin + 6
	End If
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 5, SolMargin, 6, (SagMargin-SolMargin) / 2, "HAZIRLAYAN")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 5, (SagMargin+SolMargin) / 2, 6,(SagMargin-SolMargin) / 2, "ONAYLAYAN")
	res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 3, (SagMargin+SolMargin) / 2, 30,0)
	res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 12, SolMargin, 0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Box(satirPozr + 3, SolMargin, 30,SagMargin-SolMargin)
End Sub
Function GetAlternatifTezgah(UrunKodu, AlternatifKodu)
	GetAlternatifTezgah = ""
	if trim(AlternatifKodu) = "" Then Exit Function
	QStr = "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_CODE='" & UrunKodu & "'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I'&BOMREC_ALTKODU='" & AlternatifKodu & "'"
	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("dwgt4gtgyy", "BOMU01T", QStr, "SRNUM", -1)
	if B01T.GetRowCount() <= 1 Then
		Doc.DeleteTableObject B01T.GetAppName
		Exit Function
	End if
	For i = 2 to B01T.GetRowCount
		GetAlternatifTezgah = GetAlternatifTezgah & ", " & trim(B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i))
	Next
	GetAlternatifTezgah = Mid(GetAlternatifTezgah, 3)
	Doc.DeleteTableObject B01T.GetAppName
End Function
Function GetOncekiOp(M10TI, OpSatirNo)
	GetOncekiOp = ""
	Set M10TI2 = Doc.Select2("sefef3effdr", M10TI.GetAppName, "SRNUM<'" & M10TI.SRNUM(OpSatirNo) & "'", "<SRNUM", -1)

	if M10TI2.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetOncekiOp = M10TI2.R_OPERASYON(1)
	Else
		GetOncekiOp = GetAltMPSSonOperasyon(M10TI, OpSatirNo)
	End if
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TI2.GetAppName
End Function
Function GetAltMPSSonOperasyon(M10TI, OpSatirNo)
	GetAltMPSSonOperasyon = ""

	Set M10TH1 = Doc.Select2("wedwfefrfr", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_SIRANO='" & M10TI.R_SIRANO(OpSatirNo) & "'", "", -1)
	if M10TH1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TH1.GetAppName()
		Exit Function
	End if
	For i = 1 to M10TH1.GetRowCount
		if Left(M10TH1.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), 3) <> "110" Then
			QStr = "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='UP'&KAYNAKMPS='" & M10TI.EVRAKNO(OpSatirNo) & "'&KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM='" & M10TH1.TRNUM(i) & "'"
			Set M10TM1 = Doc.Select1("dasasdasda", "MMPS10TM", QStr, "", -1)
			if M10TM1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				AltMPSNo = M10TM1.EVRAKNO(1)
				Exit For
			End if
		End if
	Next
'	msgbox AltMPSNo
	if trim(AltMPSNo) = "" Then
		Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TH1.GetAppName
		If IsObject(M10TM1) Then Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM1.GetAppName
		Exit Function
	End if
	Set M10T1 = Doc.Select1("afwefwf45r", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & AltMPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TH1.GetAppName
		Doc.DeleteTableObject M10T1.GetAppName
		If IsObject(M10TM1) Then Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM1.GetAppName
		Exit Function
	End if
	GetAltMPSSonOperasyon = M10T1.R_OPERASYON(1)
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TH1.GetAppName
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10T1.GetAppName
	If IsObject(M10TM1) Then Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM1.GetAppName
End Function
Function GetSonrakiOp(M10TI, OpSatirNo)
	GetSonrakiOp = ""
	Set M10TI2 = Doc.Select2("sefef3effdr", M10TI.GetAppName, "SRNUM>'" & M10TI.SRNUM(OpSatirNo) & "'", "SRNUM", -1)
	if M10TI2.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetSonrakiOp = M10TI2.R_OPERASYON(1)
	Else
		GetSonrakiOp = GetKaynakMPSIlkOperasyon(M10TI, OpSatirNo)
	End if
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TI2.GetAppName
End Function
Function GetKaynakMPSIlkOperasyon(M10TI, OpSatirNo)
	GetKaynakMPSIlkOperasyon = ""

	TableStr1 = "xxxdasasdasda"
	For i = 1 to MMPS10TM.GetRowCount()
		if MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(i) = "UP" Then
			KaynakMPSSiraNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_SIRANO", MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i) & MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(i), "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
			QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&R_SIRANO='" & KaynakMPSSiraNo & "'"
			Set M10T1 = Doc.Select1(TableStr1, "MMPS10T", QStr, "", -1)
			if M10T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				GetKaynakMPSIlkOperasyon = M10T1.R_OPERASYON(1)
				Doc.DeleteTableObject M10T1.GetAppName()
				Exit Function
			End if
		End if
	Next
	If IsObject(M10T1) Then Doc.DeleteTableObject M10T1.GetAppName()
End Function


Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		ncol = ncol+1
		if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
		AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
			Align = "RIGHT"
		Else
			Align = "LEFT"
		End if
		pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
		pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
			ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
			Else
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
